# 1. Concise Verdict

The authoritative Character Network Contract Field Inventory contains **430 semantic field rules** across all 32 approved contracts. This catalogue converts every rule into exactly one formal, engine-agnostic validation entry.

The catalogue provides complete Static-Checked validation coverage for directed and asymmetric relationships, endpoints, contexts, Network participation, external arrangement references, Underage and Development boundaries, Historical Memory and Reputation references, information-access pathways, visibility, provenance, derived results, saves, migration, quarantine, ownership, and deterministic processing.

The catalogue defines validation responsibilities only. It does not create executable validators, runtime types, frameworks, registries, IDs, Gameplay Tags, Unreal symbols, or content.

## Severity Summary

| Severity | Rules |
|---|---:|
| Fatal | 364 |
| Warning | 25 |
| Informational | 41 |
| **Total** | **430** |

## Severity Meaning

- **Fatal:** Reject the affected definition, initialization, command, record write, query consumption, save/load, migration step, or quarantine resolution. Never write invalid authoritative state.
- **Warning:** Preserve valid authoritative state, report the issue, and suppress unsafe derived output where appropriate. A warning never authorizes guessing or ownership transfer.
- **Informational:** Record completeness, provenance, or ordering information without blocking a valid operation or changing authoritative state.

# 3. Fatal Validation Rules

The catalogue contains **364 Fatal rules**. Fatal validation is required where failure would create duplicate ownership, corrupt directionality, invent relationships, leak hidden information, overwrite advanced saves, invalidate migration, or cause nondeterministic state.

Fatal families include:

- canonical node and external-owner resolution;
- source, target, direction, context, and arrangement validity;
- Network participation separation from Foundation lifecycle, activation, tier, and authority;
- prohibition on unsupported mirroring;
- typed-arrangement references and role separation;
- prohibition on automatic loyalty, trust, alignment, or knowledge;
- information access as pathway rather than information truth;
- Development, Underage, Historical Memory, Reputation, Trait, office, title, claim, dynasty, law, and authority boundaries;
- Opening State new-campaign-only behavior;
- save/version compatibility;
- mapping, migration, ambiguity, quarantine, and completion;
- prohibition on deprecated outputs and alias chains;
- deterministic acceptance and persistence.

A Fatal result rejects the operation, emits deterministic reasons, and preserves the last valid state. No partial authoritative mutation may commit.

# 4. Warning Validation Rules

The catalogue contains **25 Warning rules**. Warning rules identify incomplete or low-quality definitions, weak strategic purpose, missing contextual richness, or unsafe derived-output configuration that does not yet require rejection of valid authoritative state.

Warning families include:

- definitions missing useful context, visibility, source, transition, or strategic channels;
- a relationship dimension with no meaningful strategic effect;
- summary or query design that duplicates authoritative state;
- interpersonal familiarity drifting into Development familiarity;
- patronage, fealty, or obligation lacking sufficient external-source richness where formal status is not asserted;
- derived caches lacking clear invalidation;
- excessive low-value evaluation or player workload.

Warnings never authorize guessing, mirroring, ID invention, ownership transfer, or direct mutation of an external owner.

# 5. Save Validation Rules

Before save write, validate:

1. canonical node and external references;
2. direction and asymmetry;
3. context, dimensions, provenance, visibility, and participation;
4. external typed-arrangement references and compatibility versions;
5. Network information paths and contact routes;
6. owner-specific relationship states;
7. absence of duplicated Foundation, Development, Underage, Archetype, Historical Memory, Reputation, Trait, office, title, claim, dynasty, law, authority, or fact state;
8. absence of deprecated identifiers, alias chains, flat mutual legacy state, and monolithic character blobs;
9. deterministic continuation and trace metadata.

Before save load, validate save schema, Network contract version, relationship definition version, migration version, mapping version, external-arrangement compatibility, cross-specialist compatibility, migration markers, quarantine dependencies, and Opening State suppression.

A Fatal save result prevents partial mutation. Advanced save state always outranks Opening State defaults.

# 6. Migration Validation Rules

Migration validation must confirm:

1. exact source document, schema, row, field, key, or save location;
2. preserved raw source value;
3. normalization provenance;
4. canonical source and target evidence;
5. explicit direction evidence;
6. relationship context evidence;
7. external arrangement ownership and role evidence;
8. visibility, audience, and chronology evidence;
9. approved destination contract;
10. mapping and migration versions;
11. dependency order;
12. ambiguity and quarantine status;
13. owner-specific destination writes;
14. idempotent completion.

Mandatory quarantine includes unresolved identity, endpoint, direction, context, role, arrangement, source, audience, chronology, version, destination, dependency, or source semantics. Quarantine preserves data and never repairs by assumption.

# 7. Ownership Validation Rules

Validation enforces that:

- Character Network owns directed relationship state, Network contexts, relationship-side provenance, Network visibility, current access pathways, contacts, and Network-derived relational state.
- Character Foundation owns identity, lifecycle, activation, simulation tier, and person core.
- Character Development owns progress, readiness, milestones, domain familiarity, and mentorship outcomes.
- Underage Character owns guardianship, wardship, fosterage, role separation, protection, residence, education authority, and age-specific rules.
- Character Archetype owns current strategic function.
- Historical Memory owns evidence, narrative, testimony, memory, records, preservation, and legacy.
- Reputation owns current character-audience perception.
- Trait Authority owns Trait state.
- Office, Title, Claim, Succession, Dynasty, Law, Delegated Authority, Institution, Diplomacy/Coalition, and other owners retain their substantive records.
- Migration Registries and migration runtime own mappings, quarantine, and migration control.

Validators may accept, reject, diagnose, trace, or quarantine. Validators never become a second writer.

# 8. Determinism Validation Rules

Validation follows this dependency order:

1. save, content, contract, mapping, and migration versions;
2. canonical nodes and external entities;
3. Character Foundation lifecycle and Network participation;
4. external typed arrangements;
5. relationship definitions and contexts;
6. information-access and contact paths;
7. directed relationship state;
8. loyalty, trust, familiarity, patronage, fealty, obligations, and alignments;
9. visibility;
10. summaries and influence queries;
11. migration/quarantine dependencies;
12. save partition acceptance;
13. trace emission.

Within each phase, sort by canonical source, canonical target, context/type, stable record reference, chronology, observer/audience, source references, field-rule order, versions, and recorded tie breaks. Evaluate each direction independently.

Never depend on wall-clock time, frame timing, memory addresses, unordered-container iteration, asset discovery order, load order, or UI ordering. Equal state, inputs, content version, contract version, save version, migration version, mapping version, and seed must produce equal diagnostics, accept/reject outcomes, quarantine decisions, traces, and accepted authoritative state.

# 9. Risks

| Risk | Consequence | Control |
|---|---|---|
| Validator becomes another writer | Duplicate ownership | Validator accepts/rejects only |
| Validation name treated as ID | Unauthorized registry creation | Names remain descriptive labels |
| Warning used to bypass Fatal issue | Invalid state persists | Ownership, endpoint, direction, arrangement, save, and migration integrity remain Fatal |
| Flat mutual relationship accepted | Asymmetry lost | Independent directed validation |
| Access accepted as knowledge | Information-boundary failure | Source-information owner remains authoritative |
| Network reference replaces arrangement | Specialist ownership collapse | External owner and arrangement validation |
| Guardianship roles merge | Underage constitutional failure | Separate role and arrangement validation |
| Mentorship creates readiness | Development ownership bypass | Development outcome validation |
| Reputation or Memory copied into Network | Conflicting truth | References and bounded responses only |
| Trace leaks hidden data | Visibility failure | Observer-safe diagnostics and trace classification |
| Quarantine omitted | Silent migration corruption | Mandatory unresolved-data quarantine |
| Quarantine too broad | Migration stalls | Dependency graph, reason, evidence, and resolution authority |
| Validation only at save time | Invalid runtime state survives | Validate at definition, initialization, mutation, query, save, and migration boundaries |
| Unstable validation order | Divergent diagnostics and state | Fixed dependency, field-rule, and endpoint ordering |
| Missing executable evidence | False confidence | Status remains Static-Checked |

# 10. Smallest Useful Next Action

Freeze the Character Network implementation-readiness package as a Static-Checked baseline:

1. Character Network Reconciliation Audit
2. Character Network Implementation Contract Matrix
3. Character Network Contract Field Inventory
4. Character Network Validation Catalogue

Then update the migration package index to cite this baseline and mark exact identifiers, implementation symbols, serializers, save schemas, migration execution, compilation, and runtime tests as later evidence gates. No further Character Network constitutional expansion is required before moving to the next priority reconciliation or migration task.
